# Integration Server Configurations

All the server-level configurations of your Micro Integrator instance can be applied using a single configuration file, which is the `deployment.toml` file (stored in the `MI_HOME/conf` directory).

The complete list of configuration parameters that you can use in the `deployment.toml` file are listed below along with descriptions. You can also see the documentation on product [installation and setup](../install-and-setup/install-and-setup-overview.mdx) for details on applying product configurations to your Micro Integrator deployment.

## Instructions for use

To update the product configurations:

1. Open the `deployment.toml` file (stored in the `MI_HOME/conf` directory).
2. Select the required configuration headers and parameters from the list given below and apply them to the `deployment.toml` file.

The **default** `deployment.toml` file of the Micro Integrator is as follows:

```toml
[server]
hostname = "localhost"

[keystore.primary]
file_name = "wso2carbon.jks"
password = "wso2carbon"
alias = "wso2carbon"
key_password = "wso2carbon"

[truststore]
file_name = "client-truststore.jks"
password = "wso2carbon"
alias = "symmetric.key.value"
algorithm = "AES"
```

For example, if the default HTTP port is 9443 and the port offset is 1, the effective HTTP port will be 9444. ## Micro Integrator Dashboard

<div className={"mb-config-catalog"}><section><div className={"mb-config-options"}><div className={"superfences-tabs"}><input name={"4"} type={"checkbox"} id={"_tab_4"} />{" "}<label className={"tab-selector"} htmlFor={"_tab_4"}><i className={"icon fa fa-code"}></i></label><div className={"superfences-content"}><div className={"mb-config-example"}><pre><code className={"toml"}>{"[dashboard_config]\ndashboard_url = \"https://localhost:9743/dashboard/api/\"\nheartbeat_interval = 5\ngroup_id = \"mi_dev\"\nnode_id = \"dev_node_2\""}</code></pre></div></div><div className={"doc-wrapper"}><div className={"mb-config"}><div className={"config-wrap"}><code>{"[dashboard_config]"}</code>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span><p>{"This configuration header is required for the Micro Integrator server to connect with the dashboard server."}</p></div><div className={"params-wrap"}><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"dashboard_url"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"\"https://localhost:9743/dashboard/api/\""}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"https://{hostname/ip}:{port}/dashboard/api/"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The URL to access the dashboard server. Be sure to replace {hostname/ip} and {port} with the relevant values from your environment."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"heartbeat_interval"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"5"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The time interval (in seconds) between two consecutive heartbeats that are sent from the Micro Integrator to the dashboard server."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"group_id"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"default"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The server group to which the Micro Integrator instance belongs. Specify the same group ID in all the Micro Integrator servers that should belong to a single group. By default, a 'group_id' named 'default' is assinged to every Micro Integrator server that connects to the dashboard. When you sign in to the dashboard, you can view data per server group."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"node_id"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"A random UUID or the node ID used for cluster coordination."}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The dashboard identifies the Micro Integrator node by this ID. If you have already specified a node ID when you set up the Micro Integrator cluster, the same node ID applies here by default. However, if a node ID is not defined in your clustering configurations, a random uuid is used here by default."}</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section></div>


## Primary keystore

<div className={"mb-config-catalog"}><section><div className={"mb-config-options"}><div className={"superfences-tabs"}><input name={"5"} type={"checkbox"} id={"_tab_5"} />{" "}<label className={"tab-selector"} htmlFor={"_tab_5"}><i className={"icon fa fa-code"}></i></label><div className={"superfences-content"}><div className={"mb-config-example"}><pre><code className={"toml"}>{"[keystore.primary]\nfile_name = \"wso2carbon.jks\"\ntype = \"JKS\"\npassword = \"wso2carbon\"\nalias = \"wso2carbon\"\nkey_password = \"wso2carbon\""}</code></pre></div></div><div className={"doc-wrapper"}><div className={"mb-config"}><div className={"config-wrap"}><code>{"[keystore.primary]"}</code>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span><p>{"This configuration header is required for configuring the parameters that connect the Micro Integrator to the "}<a href={"/integration/micro-integrator/4.2.0/install-and-setup/setup/security/configuring-keystores/#changing-the-default-primary-keystore"}>{"primary keystore"}</a>{". This keystore is used for SSL handshaking (when the server communicates with another server) and for encrypting plain text information in configuration files. By default, this keystore is also used for encrypted data in internal datastores, unless you have configured a "}<a href={"#internal-keystore"}>{"separate keystore"}</a>{" for internal data encryption."}</p></div><div className={"params-wrap"}><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"file_name"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"wso2carbon.jks"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The name of the keystore file that is used for SSL communication and for encrypting/decrypting data in configuration files."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"type"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"JKS"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"JKS\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The type of the keystore file."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"password"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"wso2carbon"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The password of the keystore file that is used for SSL communication and for encrypting/decrypting data in configuration files. The keystore password is used when accessing the keys in the keystore."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"alias"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"wso2carbon"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The alias of the public key corresponding to the private key that is included in the keystore. The public key is used for encrypting data in the Micro Integrator server, which only the corresponding private key can decrypt. The public key is embedded in a digital certificate, and this certificate can be shared over the internet by storing it in a separate trust store file."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"key_password"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"wso2carbon"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The password of the private key that is included in the keystore. The private key is used to decrypt the data that has been encrypted using the keystore's public key."}</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section></div>


## Internal keystore

<div className={"mb-config-catalog"}><section><div className={"mb-config-options"}><div className={"superfences-tabs"}><input name={"6"} type={"checkbox"} id={"_tab_6"} />{" "}<label className={"tab-selector"} htmlFor={"_tab_6"}><i className={"icon fa fa-code"}></i></label><div className={"superfences-content"}><div className={"mb-config-example"}><pre><code className={"toml"}>{"[keystore.primary]\nfile_name = \"wso2carbon.jks\"\ntype = \"JKS\"\npassword = \"wso2carbon\"\nalias = \"wso2carbon\"\nkey_password = \"wso2carbon\""}</code></pre></div></div><div className={"doc-wrapper"}><div className={"mb-config"}><div className={"config-wrap"}><code>{"[keystore.internal]"}</code>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span><p>{"This configuration header is required for configuring the parameters that connect the Micro Integrator to the keystore used for encrypting/decrypting data in internal data stores. You may sometimes choose to configure a separate keystore for this purpose because the primary keystore needs to renew certificates frequently. However, for encrypting information in internal data stores, the keystore certificates should not be changed frequently because the data that is already encrypted will become unusable every time the certificate changes. Read more about "}<a href={"/integration/micro-integrator/4.2.0/install-and-setup/setup/security/configuring-keystores/#separating-the-internal-keystore"}>{"configuring the internal keystore"}</a>{"."}</p></div><div className={"params-wrap"}><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"file_name"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"wso2carbon.jks"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The name of the keystore file that is used for data encryption/decryption in internal data stores. By default, the keystore file of the primary keystore is enabled for this purpose."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"type"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"JKS"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"JKS\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The type of the keystore file. By default, the keystore type of the primary keystore is enabled for this purpose."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"password"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"wso2carbon"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The password of the keystore file that is used for data encryption/decryption in internal data stores. This keystore password is used when accessing the keys in the keystore. By default, the keystore password of the primary keystore is enabled for this purpose."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"alias"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"wso2carbon"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The alias of the public key corresponding to the private key that is included in the keystore. The public key is used for encrypting data in the Micro Integrator server, which only the corresponding private key can decrypt. The public key is embedded in a digital certificate, and this certificate can be shared over the internet by storing it in a separate trust store file. By default, the alias of the primary keystore is enabled for this purpose."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"key_password"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"wso2carbon"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The password of the private key that is included in the keystore. The private key is used to decrypt the data that has been encrypted using the keystore's public key. By default, the public key password of the primary keystore is enabled for this purpose."}</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section></div>


## System Parameters

<div className={"mb-config-catalog"}><section><div className={"mb-config-options"}><div className={"superfences-tabs"}><input name={"7"} type={"checkbox"} id={"_tab_7"} />{" "}<label className={"tab-selector"} htmlFor={"_tab_7"}><i className={"icon fa fa-code"}></i></label><div className={"superfences-content"}><div className={"mb-config-example"}><pre><code className={"toml"}>{"[system.parameter]\norg.wso2.SecureVaultPasswordRegEx = \"any_valid_regex\""}</code></pre></div></div><div className={"doc-wrapper"}><div className={"mb-config"}><div className={"config-wrap"}><code>{"[system.parameter]"}</code>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span><p>{"This configuration header is required for configuring system parameters for the server."}</p></div><div className={"params-wrap"}><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"org.wso2.SecureVaultPasswordRegEx"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"^[\\S]{5,30}$"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"regex value"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"A regex pattern that specifies the password length and character composition for passwords in a synapse configuration."}</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section></div>


## Truststore

<div className={"mb-config-catalog"}><section><div className={"mb-config-options"}><div className={"superfences-tabs"}><input name={"8"} type={"checkbox"} id={"_tab_8"} />{" "}<label className={"tab-selector"} htmlFor={"_tab_8"}><i className={"icon fa fa-code"}></i></label><div className={"superfences-content"}><div className={"mb-config-example"}><pre><code className={"toml"}>{"[truststore]\nfile_name=\"wso2truststore.jks\"\ntype=\"JKS\"\npassword=\"wso2carbon\"\nalias=\"symmetric.key.value\""}</code></pre></div></div><div className={"doc-wrapper"}><div className={"mb-config"}><div className={"config-wrap"}><code>{"[truststore]"}</code>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span><p>{"This configuration header is required for configuring the parameters that connect the Micro Integrator to the keystore file (trust store) that is used to store the digital certificates that the server trusts for SSL communication. Read more about "}<a href={"/integration/micro-integrator/4.2.0/install-and-setup/setup/security/configuring-keystores/#optional-changing-the-default-truststore"}>{"configuring the truststore"}</a>{"."}</p></div><div className={"params-wrap"}><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"file_name"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"wso2truststore.jks"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The name of the keystore file that is used for storing the trusted digital certificates. The product is shipped with a default trust store (wso2truststore.jks), which contains the self-signed digital certificate of the default keystore."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"type"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"JKS"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"JKS\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The type of the keystore file that is used as the trust store."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"password"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"wso2carbon"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The password of the keystore file that is used as the trust store."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"alias"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"symmetric.key.value"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The alias is the password of the digital certificate (which holds the public key) that is included in the trustore."}</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section></div>


## Default File-based User Store

<div className={"mb-config-catalog"}><section><div className={"mb-config-options"}><div className={"superfences-tabs"}><input name={"9"} type={"checkbox"} id={"_tab_9"} />{" "}<label className={"tab-selector"} htmlFor={"_tab_9"}><i className={"icon fa fa-code"}></i></label><div className={"superfences-content"}><div className={"mb-config-example"}><pre><code className={"toml"}>{"[internal_apis.file_user_store]\nenable = true\n\n[[internal_apis.users]]\nuser.name = \"user-1\"\nuser.password = \"pwd-1\"\nuser.is_admin = true\n\n[[internal_apis.users]]\nuser.name = \"user-2\"\nuser.password = \"pwd-2\"\n"}</code></pre></div></div><div className={"doc-wrapper"}><div className={"mb-config"}><div className={"config-wrap"}><code>{"[internal_apis.file_user_store]"}</code>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span><p>{"This configuration header is required for disabling the default file-based user store of the Micro Integrator's Management API. Read more about "}<a href={"/integration/micro-integrator/4.2.0/install-and-setup/setup/user-stores/setting-up-a-userstore/"}>{"configuring user stores"}</a>{"."}</p></div><div className={"params-wrap"}><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"enable"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"true"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"true\" or \"false\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Set this paramter to 'false' if you want to disable the default file-based user store. This allows you to use an external user store for user authentication in the Management API."}</p></div></div></div></div><div className={"config-wrap"}><code>{"[[internal_apis.users]]"}</code>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span><p>{"This configuration header is required for defining the user name and password for the Management API. Reuse this header when you want to add more users. The user credentials are stored in the default file-based user store of the Management API. Read more about "}<a href={"/integration/micro-integrator/4.2.0/install-and-setup/setup/user-stores/setting-up-a-userstore/"}>{"configuring user stores"}</a>{"."}</p></div><div className={"params-wrap"}><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"user.name"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"admin"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Enter a user name. Note that this will overwrite the default 'admin' user that is stored in the user store."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"user.password"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"admin"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Enter a password for the user specified by 'user.name'. Note that this will overwrite the default 'admin' password that is stored in the user store."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"user.is_admin"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" boolean "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"false"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"true\" or \"false\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Specifies whether or not the user has admin privileges."}</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section></div>


## External User Store

<div className={"mb-config-catalog"}><section><div className={"mb-config-options"}><div className={"superfences-tabs"}><input name={"10"} type={"checkbox"} id={"_tab_10"} />{" "}<label className={"tab-selector"} htmlFor={"_tab_10"}><i className={"icon fa fa-code"}></i></label><div className={"superfences-content"}><div className={"mb-config-example"}><pre><code className={"toml"}>{"[user_store]\ntype = \"read_only_ldap\"\nclass = \"org.wso2.micro.integrator.security.user.core.ldap.ReadOnlyLDAPUserStoreManager\"\nconnection_url = \"ldap://localhost:10389\"\nconnection_name = \"uid=admin,ou=system\"\nconnection_password = \"admin\"\nanonymous_bind = false\nuser_search_base = \"ou=Users,dc=wso2,dc=org\"\nuser_name_attribute = \"uid\"\nuser_name_search_filter = \"(&(objectClass=person)(uid=?))\"\nuser_name_list_filter = \"(objectClass=person)\"\nread_groups = true\ngroup_search_base = \"ou=Groups,dc=wso2,dc=org\"\ngroup_name_attribute = \"cn\"\ngroup_name_search_filter = \"(&(objectClass=groupOfNames)(cn=?))\"\ngroup_name_list_filter = \"(objectClass=groupOfNames)\"\nmembership_attribute = \"member\"\nback_links_enabled = false\nusername_java_regex = \"[a-zA-Z0-9._\\\\-|//]{3,30}$\"\nrolename_java_regex = \"[a-zA-Z0-9._\\\\-|//]{3,30}$\"\npassword_java_regex = \"^[\\\\S]{5,30}$\"\nscim_enabled = false\npassword_hash_method = \"PLAIN_TEXT\"\nmulti_attribute_separator = \",\"\nmax_user_name_list_length = 100\nmax_role_name_list_length = 100\nuser_roles_cache_enabled = true\nconnection_pooling_enabled = true\nldap_connection_timeout = 5000\nread_timeout = ''\nretry_attempts = ''\nconnection_retry_delay = \"120000\"\n"}</code></pre></div></div><div className={"doc-wrapper"}><div className={"mb-config"}><div className={"config-wrap"}><code>{"[user_store]"}</code>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span><p>{"This configuration header is required for conencting the Micro Integrator to an "}<a href={"/integration/micro-integrator/4.2.0/install-and-setup/setup/user-stores/setting-up-a-userstore/"}>{"external user store"}</a>{"."}</p></div><div className={"params-wrap"}><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"type"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"\"read_only_ldap\""}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"read_only_ldap\", \"read_write_ldap\", \"database\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"This parameter specifies the type of user store. The following options are available: <ul><li>read_only_ldap: The Micro Integrator connects to a read-only LDAP. </li><li>read_write_ldap: The Micro Integrator connects to an LDAP with write permissions.</li><li>database: The Micro Integrator connects to an RDBMS user store.</li></ul> When you set this parameter, all of the remaining parameters (listed below) are inferred with default values. You can override the defaults by giving specific values to these parameters."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"class"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"org.wso2.micro.integrator.security.user.core.ldap.ReadOnlyLDAPUserStoreManager"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The implementation class that enables the read-only LDAP user store. If the type parameter is not used, you need to specify a value for this parameter."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"read_only"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" boolean "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"true"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"true\" or \"false\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Specifies whether or not the user store is read only."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"connection_url"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"ldap://localhost:10389"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The URL for connecting to the LDAP. Override the default URL for your setup. If you are connecting over ldaps (secured LDAP), you need to import the certificate of the user store to the truststore (wso2truststore.jks by default). See the instructions on how to "}<a href={"/integration/micro-integrator/4.2.0/install-and-setup/setup/security/importing-ssl-certificate/"}>{"add certificates to the truststore"}</a>{"."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"connection_name"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"uid=admin,ou=system"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The username used to connect to the user store and perform various operations. This user does not need to be an administrator in the user store. However, the user requires permission to read the user list and user attributes, and to perform search operations on the user store. The value you specify is used as the DN (Distinguish Name) attribute of the user who has sufficient permissions to perform operations on users and roles in LDAP."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"connection_password"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"admin"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Password for the connection user name."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"user_search_base"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"ou=system"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The DN of the context or object under which the user entries are stored in the user store. When the user store searches for users, it will start from this location of the directory."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"user_name_attribute"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"uid"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The attribute used for uniquely identifying a user entry. Users can be authenticated using their email address, UID, etc. The name of the attribute is considered as the username. Note that the email address is considered as a special case in WSO2 products. Read more about using the email address as user name."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"user_name_search_filter"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"(&amp;(objectClass=person)(uid=?))"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Filtering criteria used to search for a particular user entry."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"user_name_list_filter"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"(objectClass=person)"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Filtering criteria for searching user entries in the user store. This query or filter is used when doing search operations on users with different search attributes. According to the default configuration, the search operation only provides the objects created from the person object class."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"read_groups"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" boolean "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"true"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"true\" or \"false\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"This indicates whether groups should be read from the user store. If this is set to 'false', none of the groups in the user store can be read, and the following group configurations are NOT mandatory: 'group_search_base', 'group_name_list_filter', or 'group_name_attribute'."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"group_search_base"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"ou=system"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The DN of the context or object under which the group entries are stored in the user store. When the user store searches for groups, it will start from this location of the directory."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"group_name_attribute"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"cn"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The attribute used for uniquely identifying a group entry. This attribute is to be treated as the group name."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"group_name_search_filter"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"(&amp;(objectClass=groupOfNames)(cn=?))"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The filtering criteria used to search for a particular group entry."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"group_name_list_filter"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"(objectClass=groupOfNames)"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The filtering criteria for searching group entries in the user store. This query or filter is used when doing search operations on groups with different search attributes."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"membership_attribute"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"member"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Defines the attribute that contains the distinguished names (DN) of user objects that are in a group."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"back_links_enabled"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"member"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Defines whether the backlink support is enabled."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"username_java_regex"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"[a-zA-Z0-9._\\-|//]{3,30}$"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The regular expression used by the back-end components for username validation. By default, a length of 3 to 30 allowed for strings with non-empty characters. You can provide ranges of alphabets, numbers, and also ranges of ASCII values in the RegEx properties."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"rolename_java_regex"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"[a-zA-Z0-9._\\-|//]{3,30}$"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The regular expression used by the back-end components for role name validation. By default, a length of 3 to 30 allowed for strings with non-empty characters. You can provide ranges of alphabets, numbers, and also ranges of ASCII values in the RegEx properties."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"password_java_regex"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"^[\\S]{5,30}$"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The regular expression used by the back-end components for password validation. By default, a length of 3 to 30 allowed for strings with non-empty characters. You can provide ranges of alphabets, numbers, and also ranges of ASCII values in the RegEx properties."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"scim_enabled"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" boolean "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"true"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"true\" or \"false\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The regular expression used by the back-end components for password validation. By default, a length of 3 to 30 allowed for strings with non-empty characters. You can provide ranges of alphabets, numbers, and also ranges of ASCII values in the RegEx properties."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"password_hash_method"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"PLAIN_TEXT"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"SHA\", \"MD5\", \"PLAIN_TEXT\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Specifies the password hashing algorithm used for hashing the password before storing in the user store. You can use the SHA digest method (SHA-1, SHA-256), the MD 5 digest method, or plain text passwords."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"multi_attribute_separator"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{","}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"This parameter is used to define a character to separate multiple attributes. This ensures that it will not appear as part of a claim value. Normally ',' is used to separate multiple attributes, but you can define ',,,', '...', or a similar character sequence."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"max_user_name_list_length"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"100"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Controls the number of users listed in the user store. This is useful when you have a large number of users and you don't want to list them all. Setting this property to 0 displays all users. In some user stores, there are policies to limit the number of records that can be returned from the query. Setting the value to 0 will list the maximum results returned by the user store. To increase that value, you need to set it at the user store level. Active directory has the 'MaxPageSize' property with the default value set to 1000."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"max_role_name_list_length"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"100"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Controls the number of roles listed in the user store. This is useful when you have a large number of roles and you don't want to list them all. Setting this property to 0 displays all roles. In some user stores, there are policies to limit the number of records that can be returned from the query. Setting the value to 0 will list the maximum results returned by the user store. To increase that value, you need to set it at the user store level. Active directory has the 'MaxPageSize' property with the default value set to 1000."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"user_roles_cache_enabled"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" boolean "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"true"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"true\" or \"false\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"This parameter indicates whether the list of roles for a user should be cached. Set this to 'false' if the user roles are changed by external means and the changes should be instantly reflected in the product instance."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"connection_pooling_enabled"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" boolean "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"true"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"true\" or \"false\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Define whether LDAP connection pooling is enabled. The connection performance will improve when this parameter is enabled."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"ldap_connection_timeout"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"5000"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"This is the connection timeout period (in milliseconds) when the initial connection is created."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"read_timeout"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The value for this parameter is the read timeout in milliseconds for LDAP operations. If the LDAP provider cannot get an LDAP response within that period, it aborts the read attempt. The integer should be greater than zero. An integer less than or equal to zero means no read timeout is specified, which is equivalent to waiting for the response infinitely until it is received."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"retry_attempts"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Retry the authentication request if a timeout happened."}</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section></div>


## Database Connection

<div className={"mb-config-catalog"}><section><div className={"mb-config-options"}><div className={"superfences-tabs"}><input name={"11"} type={"checkbox"} id={"_tab_11"} />{" "}<label className={"tab-selector"} htmlFor={"_tab_11"}><i className={"icon fa fa-code"}></i></label><div className={"superfences-content"}><div className={"mb-config-example"}><pre><code className={"toml"}>{"[[datasource]]\nid = \"WSO2_CARBON_DB\"\nurl= \"jdbc:h2:./repository/database/WSO2CARBON_DB;DB_CLOSE_ON_EXIT=FALSE;LOCK_TIMEOUT=60000\"\nusername=\"username\"\npassword=\"password\"\ndriver=\"org.h2.Driver\"\npool_options.maxActive=50\npool_options.maxWait = 60000\npool_options.testOnBorrow = true"}</code></pre></div></div><div className={"doc-wrapper"}><div className={"mb-config"}><div className={"config-wrap"}><code>{"[[datasource]]"}</code>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span><p>{"This configuration header is required for connecting to a database from the Micro Integrator. Databases are only required if you are connecting the Micro Integrator to an "}<a href={"/integration/micro-integrator/4.2.0/install-and-setup/setup/user-stores/setting-up-a-userstore/#configuring-an-rdbms-user-store"}>{"RDBMS user store"}</a>{"."}</p></div><div className={"params-wrap"}><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"id"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The name of the database."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"url"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The connection URL for your database. Note that the URL depends on the type of database you use."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"username"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The user name for connecting to the database."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"password"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The password for connecting to the database."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"driver"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The driver class of your database."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"pool_options.maxActive"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"50"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The maximum number of active connections that can be allocated from this pool at the same time. If you set this value too low, the response times for some requests might slow down as they have to wait for connections to get free. A value too high might cause too much memory/resource utilization and the system may slow down or be unresponsive."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"pool_options.maxWait"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"60000"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Maximum number of milliseconds that the pool waits (when there are no available connections) for a connection to be returned before throwing an exception."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"pool_options.testOnBorrow"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" boolean "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"true"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"true\" or \"false\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Used to indicate if objects will be validated before being borrowed from the pool. If the object fails to validate, it will be dropped from the pool, and we will attempt to borrow another one."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"pool_options.maxIdle"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"8"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The maximum number of connections that can remain idle in the pool, without extra ones being released. Default value is 8. Put a negative value for unlimited. Idle connections are checked periodically (if enabled) and connections that have been idle for longer than minEvictableIdleTimeMillis will be released."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"pool_options.minIdle"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"0"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The minimum number of connections that can remain idle in the pool, without extra ones being created. The connection pool can shrink below this number if validation queries fail. Default value is 0."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"pool_options.validationInterval"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"30000"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"This parameter controls how frequently a given validation query is executed (time in milliseconds). The default value is 30000 (30 seconds). That is, if a connection is due for validation, but has been validated previously within this interval, it will not be validated again."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"pool_options.validationQuery"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"Null"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The SQL query used to validate connections from this pool before returning them to the caller. If specified, this query does not have to return any data, it just can't throw an SQLException. The default value is null. Example values are SELECT 1(mysql), select 1 from dual(oracle), SELECT 1(MS Sql Server)."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"pool_options.MaxPermSize"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The memory size allocated for WSO2 Micro Integrator."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"pool_options.removeAbandoned"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" boolean "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"false"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"true\" or \"false\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"If this property is set to 'true', a connection is considered abandoned and eligible for removal if it has been in use for longer than the removeAbandonedTimeout value explained below."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"pool_options.removeAbandonedTimeout"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" boolean "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"false"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"true\" or \"false\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The time in seconds that should pass before a connection that is in use can be removed. This is the time period after which the connection will be declared abandoned. This value should be set to the longest running query that the applications might have."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"pool_options.logAbandoned"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" boolean "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"false"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"true\" or \"false\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Set this property to 'true' if you wish to log when the connection was abandoned. If this option is set to 'true', a stack trace is recorded during the dataSource.getConnection call and is printed when a connection is not returned."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"pool_options.initialSize"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"0"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The initial number of connections created when the pool is started. Default value is 0."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"pool_options.defaultTransactionIsolation"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"TRANSACTION_NONE"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"TRANSACTION_NONE\", \"TRANSACTION_UNKNOWN\", \"TRANSACTION_READ_COMMITTED\", \"TRANSACTION_READ_UNCOMMITTED\", \"TRANSACTION_REPEATABLE_READ\", \"TRANSACTION_SERIALIZABLE\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The default TransactionIsolation state of connections created by this pool."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"pool_options.validationQueryTimeout"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-1"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The timeout in seconds before a connection validation queries fail. This works by calling java.sql.Statement.setQueryTimeout(seconds) on the statement that executes the validationQuery . The pool itself doesn't timeout the query. It is still up to the JDBC driver to enforce query timeouts. A value less than or equal to zero will disable this feature. The default value is -1."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"pool_options.timeBetweenEvictionRunsMillis"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"5000"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The number of milliseconds to sleep between runs of the idle connection validation/cleaner thread. This value should not be set under 1 second. It dictates how often we check for idle, abandoned connections, and how often we validate idle connections. The default value is 5000 (5 seconds)."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"pool_options.numTestsPerEvictionRun"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The number of objects to examine during each run of the idle object evictor thread."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"pool_options.minEvictableIdleTimeMillis"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"60000"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The minimum amount of time an object may sit idle in the pool before it is eligible for eviction. The default value is 60000 (60 seconds)."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"pool_options.defaultCatalog"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The default catalog of connections created by this pool."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"pool_options.validatorClassName"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The name of a class that implements the org.apache.tomcat.jdbc.pool.Validator interface and provides a no-arg constructor (may be implicit). If specified, the class will be used to create a Validator instance, which is then used instead of any validation query to validate connections. The default value is null. An example value is com.mycompany.project.SimpleValidator."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"pool_options.connectionProperties"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"Null"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The connection properties that will be sent to our JDBC driver when establishing new connections. Format of the string must be [propertyName=property;]* NOTE - The 'user' and 'password' properties will be passed explicitly, so they do not need to be included here. The default value is null."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"pool_options.initSQL"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The ability to run a SQL statement exactly once, when the connection is created."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"pool_options.jdbcInterceptors"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Flexible and pluggable interceptors to create any customizations around the pool, the query execution and the result set handling."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"pool_options.abandonWhenPercentageFull"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"0"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Connections that have been abandoned (timed out) wont get closed and reported up unless the number of connections in use are above the percentage defined by abandonWhenPercentageFull. The value should be between 0-100. The default value is 0, which implies that connections are eligible for closure as soon as removeAbandonedTimeout has been reached."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"pool_options.maxAge"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"0"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Time in milliseconds to keep this connection. When a connection is returned to the pool, the pool will check to see if the now - time-when-connected > maxAge has been reached, and if so, it closes the connection rather than returning it to the pool. The default value is 0, which implies that connections will be left open and no age check will be done upon returning the connection to the pool."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"pool_options.suspectTimeout"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"0"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Timeout value in seconds. Default value is 0. Similar to to the removeAbandonedTimeout value but instead of treating the connection as abandoned, and potentially closing the connection, this simply logs the warning if logAbandoned is set to true. If this value is equal or less than 0, no suspect checking will be performed. Suspect checking only takes place if the timeout value is larger than 0 and the connection was not abandoned or if abandon check is disabled. If a connection is suspect a WARN message gets logged and a JMX notification gets sent once."}</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section></div>


## Management API - JWT Handler

<div className={"mb-config-catalog"}><section><div className={"mb-config-options"}><div className={"superfences-tabs"}><input name={"12"} type={"checkbox"} id={"_tab_12"} />{" "}<label className={"tab-selector"} htmlFor={"_tab_12"}><i className={"icon fa fa-code"}></i></label><div className={"superfences-content"}><div className={"mb-config-example"}><pre><code className={"toml"}>{"[management_api.jwt_token_security_handler]\nenable = true\ntoken_store_config.max_size= \"200\"\ntoken_store_config.clean_up_interval= \"600\"\ntoken_store_config.remove_oldest_token_on_overflow= \"true\"\ntoken_config.expiry= \"3600\"\ntoken_config.size= \"2048\"\n"}</code></pre></div></div><div className={"doc-wrapper"}><div className={"mb-config"}><div className={"config-wrap"}><code>{"[management_api.jwt_token_security_handler]"}</code>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span><p>{"This configuration header is required for configuring the default JWT token store configurations of the Micro Integrator's Management API.
