Read more about "}<a href={"../../install-and-setup/setup/security/securing-management-api"}>{"securing the Management API"}</a>{"."}</p></div><div className={"params-wrap"}><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"enable"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" boolean "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"true"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"true\" or \"false\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Set this paramter to 'false' if you want to disable JWT authentication for the management API."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"token_store_config.max_size"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"200"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Number of tokens stored in the in-memory token store. User can increase or decrease this value accordingly."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"token_store_config.clean_up_interval"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"600"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Token cleanup will be handled through a seperate thread and the frequency of the token clean up can be configured from this setting. This will clean all the expired and revoked security tokens. The thread will run only when there are tokens in the store. If it is empty, the cleanup thread will automatically stop. Interval is specified in seconds."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"token_store_config.remove_oldest_token_on_overflow"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" boolean "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"true"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"true\" or \"false\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"If set to 'true', this will remove the oldest accessed token when the token store is full. If it is set to 'false', the user should either wait until other tokens expire or increase the token store max size accordingly."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"token_config.expiry"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"3600"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"This configures the expiry time of the token (specified in seconds)."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"token_config.size"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"2048"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Specifies the key size of the token."}</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section></div>


## Management API - Authorization Handler

<div className={"mb-config-catalog"}><section><div className={"mb-config-options"}><div className={"superfences-tabs"}><input name={"13"} type={"checkbox"} id={"_tab_13"} />{" "}<label className={"tab-selector"} htmlFor={"_tab_13"}><i className={"icon fa fa-code"}></i></label><div className={"superfences-content"}><div className={"mb-config-example"}><pre><code className={"toml"}>{"[management_api.authorization_handler]\nenable = false\n\n[[management_api.authorization_handler.resources]]\npath = \"/users\"\n\n[[management_api.authorization_handler.resources]]\npath = \"/apis\"\n"}</code></pre></div></div><div className={"doc-wrapper"}><div className={"mb-config"}><div className={"config-wrap"}><code>{"[management_api.authorization_handler]"}</code>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span><p>{"This configuration header is required for disabling authorization for the Micro Integrator's Management API. Authorization only applies when an external user store is used. Read more about "}<a href={"../../install-and-setup/setup/security/securing-management-api"}>{"securing the Management API"}</a>{"."}</p></div><div className={"params-wrap"}><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"enable"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" boolean "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"true"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"true\" or \"false\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Set this paramter to 'false' if you want to disable authorization for the management API."}</p></div></div></div></div><div className={"config-wrap"}><code>{"[[management_api.authorization_handler.resources]]"}</code>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span><p>{"This configuration header is required for enabling authorization for additional resources (other than 'users') of the Micro Integrator's Management API. Read more about "}<a href={"../../install-and-setup/setup/security/securing-management-api"}>{"securing the Management API"}</a>{"."}</p></div><div className={"params-wrap"}><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"path"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code></code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"/resource_name"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Use this parameter to specify the resources in the management API for which you want to enable authorization."}</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section></div>


## Management API - CORS

<div className={"mb-config-catalog"}><section><div className={"mb-config-options"}><div className={"superfences-tabs"}><input name={"14"} type={"checkbox"} id={"_tab_14"} />{" "}<label className={"tab-selector"} htmlFor={"_tab_14"}><i className={"icon fa fa-code"}></i></label><div className={"superfences-content"}><div className={"mb-config-example"}><pre><code className={"toml"}>{"[management_api.cors]\nenabled = true\nallowed_origins = \"*\"\nallowed_headers = \"Authorization\""}</code></pre></div></div><div className={"doc-wrapper"}><div className={"mb-config"}><div className={"config-wrap"}><code>{"[management_api.cors]"}</code>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span><p>{"This configuration header is required for configuring CORs for the Management API of the Micro Integrator. Read more about "}<a href={"../../install-and-setup/setup/security/securing-management-api"}>{"securing the Management API"}</a>{"."}</p></div><div className={"params-wrap"}><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"enabled"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" boolean "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"true"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Set this paramter to 'false' if you want to disable CORs for the Management API."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"allowed_origins"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"*"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"any string"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Specify the allowed origins. By default '*' indicates that all origins are allowed."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"allowed_headers"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"Authorization"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Specify the allowed authorization headers."}</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section></div>


## Message Builders (non-blocking mode)

<div className={"mb-config-catalog"}><section><div className={"mb-config-options"}><div className={"superfences-tabs"}><input name={"15"} type={"checkbox"} id={"_tab_15"} />{" "}<label className={"tab-selector"} htmlFor={"_tab_15"}><i className={"icon fa fa-code"}></i></label><div className={"superfences-content"}><div className={"mb-config-example"}><pre><code className={"toml"}>{"[message_builders]\napplication_xml = \"org.apache.axis2.builder.ApplicationXMLBuilder\"\nform_urlencoded = \"org.apache.synapse.commons.builders.XFormURLEncodedBuilder\"\nmultipart_form_data = \"org.apache.axis2.builder.MultipartFormDataBuilder\"\ntext_plain = \"org.apache.axis2.format.PlainTextBuilder\"\napplication_json = \"org.wso2.micro.integrator.core.json.JsonStreamBuilder\"\njson_badgerfish = \"org.apache.axis2.json.JSONBadgerfishOMBuilder\"\ntext_javascript = \"org.apache.axis2.json.JSONBuilder\"\noctet_stream =  \"org.wso2.carbon.relay.BinaryRelayBuilder\"\napplication_binary = \"org.apache.axis2.format.BinaryBuilder\""}</code></pre></div></div><div className={"doc-wrapper"}><div className={"mb-config"}><div className={"config-wrap"}><code>{"[message_builders]"}</code>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span><p>{"This configuration header is required for configuring the "}<a href={"../../install-and-setup/setup/message-builders-formatters/message-builders-and-formatters"}>{"message builder"}</a>{" implementation that is used to build messages that are received by the Micro Integrator in the default non-blocking mode. If you are using the Micro Integrator in blocking mode, see the "}<a href={"#message-builders-blocking-mode"}>{"message builder configurations for blocking mode"}</a>{"."}</p></div><div className={"params-wrap"}><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"application_xml"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"org.apache.axis2.builder.ApplicationXMLBuilder"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The message builder implementation that builds messages with the 'application_xml' content type. If required, you can change the default builder class."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"text_plain"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"org.apache.axis2.format.PlainTextBuilder"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The message builder implementation that builds messages with the 'text_plain' content type. If required, you can change the default builder class."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"application_json"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"org.wso2.micro.integrator.core.json.JsonStreamBuilder"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The message builder implementation that builds messages with the 'application_json' content type. ## Message Builders (blocking mode)

<div className={"mb-config-catalog"}><section><div className={"mb-config-options"}><div className={"superfences-tabs"}><input name={"16"} type={"checkbox"} id={"_tab_16"} />{" "}<label className={"tab-selector"} htmlFor={"_tab_16"}><i className={"icon fa fa-code"}></i></label><div className={"superfences-content"}><div className={"mb-config-example"}><pre><code className={"toml"}>{"[blocking.message_builders]\napplication_xml = \"org.apache.axis2.builder.ApplicationXMLBuilder\"\nform_urlencoded = \"org.apache.synapse.commons.builders.XFormURLEncodedBuilder\"\nmultipart_form_data = \"org.apache.axis2.builder.MultipartFormDataBuilder\"\ntext_plain = \"org.apache.axis2.format.PlainTextBuilder\"\napplication_json = \"org.wso2.micro.integrator.core.json.JsonStreamBuilder\"\njson_badgerfish = \"org.apache.axis2.json.JSONBadgerfishOMBuilder\"\ntext_javascript = \"org.apache.axis2.json.JSONBuilder\"\noctet_stream =  \"org.wso2.carbon.relay.BinaryRelayBuilder\"\napplication_binary = \"org.apache.axis2.format.BinaryBuilder\""}</code></pre></div></div><div className={"doc-wrapper"}><div className={"mb-config"}><div className={"config-wrap"}><code>{"[blocking.message_builders]"}</code>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span><p>{"This configuration header is required for configuring the "}<a href={"../../install-and-setup/setup/message-builders-formatters/message-builders-and-formatters"}>{"message builder"}</a>{" implementation that is used to build messages that are received by the Micro Integrator in "}<b>{"blocking"}</b>{" mode. You can use the "}<a href={"#message-builders-non-blocking-mode"}>{"same list of parameters"}</a>{" that are available for message builders in non-blocking mode."}</p></div><div className={"params-wrap"}></div></div></div></div></div></section></div>

See the instructions on configuring "}<a href={"../../install-and-setup/setup/message-builders-formatters/message-builders-and-formatters"}>{"custom message builders and formatters"}</a>{"."}</p></div><div className={"params-wrap"}><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"content_type"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The content types to which the custom message builder implementation should apply. You can specify the list of content types as follows: application/json/badgerfish."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"class"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The custom message builder implementation that should apply to the given content types."}</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section></div>

## Server Request Processor

<div className={"mb-config-catalog"}><section><div className={"mb-config-options"}><div className={"superfences-tabs"}><input name={"23"} type={"checkbox"} id={"_tab_23"} />{" "}<label className={"tab-selector"} htmlFor={"_tab_23"}><i className={"icon fa fa-code"}></i></label><div className={"superfences-content"}><div className={"mb-config-example"}><pre><code className={"toml"}>{"[[server.get_request_processor]]\nitem = \"swagger.yaml\"\nclass = \"org.wso2.micro.integrator.transport.handlers.requestprocessors.swagger.format.SwaggerYamlProcessor\"\n\n[[server.get_request_processor]]\nitem = \"swagger.json\"\nclass = \"org.wso2.micro.integrator.transport.handlers.requestprocessors.swagger.format.SwaggerJsonProcessor\""}</code></pre></div></div><div className={"doc-wrapper"}><div className={"mb-config"}><div className={"config-wrap"}><code>{"[[server.get_request_processor]]"}</code>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span><p>{"This configuration header is required for configuring the parameters that specify how special HTTP GET requests (such as '?wsdl', '?policy', etc.) are processed. This is an array-type header, which you can reuse depending on the number of processors you want to enable."}</p></div><div className={"params-wrap"}><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"item"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"\"swagger.yaml\" and \"swagger.json\""}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The item repesents the first parameter in the query string (e.g. ?wsdl), which needs special processing."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"class"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"\"org.wso2.micro.integrator.transport.handlers.requestprocessors.swagger.format.SwaggerYamlProcessor\" and \"org.wso2.micro.integrator.transport.handlers.requestprocessors.swagger.format.SwaggerYamlProcessor\""}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"This is the class that implements the org.wso2.carbon.transport.HttpGetRequestProcessor processor. By default, the following two classes are used for handling the two default request items: org.wso2.micro.integrator.transport.handlers.requestprocessors.swagger.format.SwaggerYamlProcessor (for swagger.yaml) and org.wso2.micro.integrator.transport.handlers.requestprocessors.swagger.format.SwaggerYamlProcessor (for swagger.json)."}</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section></div>


## HTTP/S transport (non-blocking mode)

<div className={"mb-config-catalog"}><section><div className={"mb-config-options"}><div className={"superfences-tabs"}><input name={"24"} type={"checkbox"} id={"_tab_24"} />{" "}<label className={"tab-selector"} htmlFor={"_tab_24"}><i className={"icon fa fa-code"}></i></label><div className={"superfences-content"}><div className={"mb-config-example"}><pre><code className={"toml"}>{"[transport.http]\nsocket_timeout = \"3m\"\ncore_worker_pool_size = 400\nmax_worker_pool_size = 400\nworker_pool_queue_length = -1\nio_buffer_size = 16384\nmax_http_connection_per_host_port = 32767\npreserve_http_user_agent = false\npreserve_http_server_name = true\npreserve_http_headers = [\"Content-Type\"]\ndisable_connection_keepalive = false\nenable_message_size_validation = false\nmax_message_size_bytes = 81920\nmax_open_connections = -1\nforce_xml_validation = false\nforce_json_validation = false\nlistener.port = 8280    #inferred  default: 8280\nlistener.wsdl_epr_prefix =\"$ref{server.hostname}\"\nlistener.bind_address = \"$ref{server.hostname}\"\nlistener.secured_port = 8243\nlistener.secured_wsdl_epr_prefix = \"$ref{server.hostname}\"\nlistener.secured_bind_address = \"$ref{server.hostname}\"\nlistener.secured_protocols = \"TLSv1,TLSv1.1,TLSv1.2\"\nlistener.verify_client = \"require\"\nlistener.ssl_profile.file_path = \"conf/sslprofiles/listenerprofiles.xml\"\nlistener.ssl_profile.read_interval = \"1h\"\nlistener.preferred_ciphers = \"TLS_ECDHE_ECDSA_WITH_AES_128_CBC_SHA256,TLS_ECDHE_RSA_WITH_AES_128_CBC_SHA256,TLS_DHE_RSA_WITH_AES_128_CBC_SHA256,TLS_ECDHE_ECDSA_WITH_AES_128_CBC_SHA,TLS_ECDHE_RSA_WITH_AES_128_CBC_SHA,TLS_DHE_RSA_WITH_AES_128_CBC_SHA,TLS_ECDHE_ECDSA_WITH_AES_128_GCM_SHA256,TLS_ECDHE_RSA_WITH_AES_128_GCM_SHA256,TLS_DHE_RSA_WITH_AES_128_GCM_SHA256\"\nlistener.keystore.location =\"repository/resources/security/wso2carbon.jks\"\nlistener.keystore.type = \"JKS\" \nlistener.keystore.password = \"wso2carbon\"\nlistener.keystore.key_password = \"wso2carbon\"\nlistener.truststore.location = \"repository/resources/security/client-truststore.jks\"\nlistener.truststore.type = \"JKS\"\nlistener.truststore.password = \"wso2carbon\"\nsender.warn_on_http_500 = \"*\"\nsender.proxy_host = \"$ref{server.hostname}\"\nsender.proxy_port = 3128\nsender.non_proxy_hosts = [\"$ref{server.hostname}\"]\nsender.hostname_verifier = \"AllowAll\"\nsender.keystore.location =\"repository/resources/security/wso2carbon.jks\"\nsender.keystore.type = \"JKS\"\nsender.keystore.password = \"wso2carbon\"\nsender.keystore.key_password = \"wso2carbon\"\nsender.truststore.location = \"repository/resources/security/client-truststore.jks\"\nsender.truststore.type = \"JKS\"\nsender.truststore.password = \"wso2carbon\"\nsender.ssl_profile.file_path = \"conf/sslprofiles/senderprofiles.xml\"\nsender.ssl_profile.read_interval = \"30s\"\nenable_message_size_validation = false\nmax_message_size_bytes = 2147483647\nmax_open_connections = -1\nforce_xml_validation = false\nforce_json_validation = false"}</code></pre></div></div><div className={"doc-wrapper"}><div className={"mb-config"}><div className={"config-wrap"}><code>{"[transport.http]"}</code>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span><p>{"This configuration header is required for configuring the parameters that are used for "}<a href={"../../install-and-setup/setup/performance-tuning/http-transport-tuning"}>{"tuning the default HTTP/S passthrough transport"}</a>{" of the Micro Integrator in non-blocking mode."}</p></div><div className={"params-wrap"}><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"socket_timeout"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"180000"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"This is the maximum period of inactivity between two consecutive data packets, specified in milliseconds."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"core_worker_pool_size"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"400"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The Micro Integrator uses a thread pool executor to create threads and to handle incoming requests. This parameter controls the number of core threads used by the executor pool. If you increase this parameter value, the number of requests received that can be processed by the integrator increases, hence, the throughput also increases. The nature of the integration scenario and the number of concurrent requests received by the integrator are the main factors that helps to determine this parameter."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"max_worker_pool_size"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"400"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"This is the maximum number of threads in the worker thread pool. Specifying a maximum limit avoids performance degradation that can occur due to context switching. If the specified value is reached, you will see the error 'SYSTEM ALERT - HttpServerWorker threads were in BLOCKED state during last minute'. This can occur due to an extraordinarily high number of requests sent at a time when all the threads in the pool are busy, and the maximum number of threads is already reached."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"worker_pool_queue_length"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-1"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"This defines the length of the queue that is used to hold runnable tasks to be executed by the worker pool. The thread pool starts queuing jobs when all the existing threads are busy, and the pool has reached the maximum number of threads. The value for this parameter should be -1 to use an unbound queue. If a bound queue is used and the queue gets filled to its capacity, any further attempts to submit jobs fail causing some messages to be dropped by Synapse."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"io_buffer_size"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"16384"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"This is the value of the memory buffer allocated when reading data into the memory from the underlying socket/file channels. You should leave this property set to the default value."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"max_http_connection_per_host_port"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"32767"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"This defines the maximum number of connections allowed per host port."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"preserve_http_user_agent"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" boolean "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"\"true\" or \"false\""}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"If this parameter is set to true, the user-agent HTTP header of messages passing through the integrator is preserved and printed in the outgoing message."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"preserve_http_headers"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"Content-Type"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"This parameter allows you to specify the header field/s of messages passing through the EI that need to be preserved and printed in the outgoing message such as Location, CommonsHTTPTransportSenderKeep-Alive, Date, Server, User-Agent, and Host. For example, http.headers.preserve = Location, Date, Server."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"disable_connection_keepalive"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" boolean "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"false"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"true\" or \"false\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"If this parameter is set to true, the HTTP connections with the back end service are closed soon after the request is served. It is recommended to set this property to false so that the integrator does not have to create a new connection every time it sends a request to a back-end service. However, you may need to close connections after they are used if the back-end service does not provide sufficient support for keep-alive connections."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"listener.port"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"8290"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The port on which this transport receiver should listen for incoming messages."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"listener.wsdl_epr_prefix"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span "}<code>{"MI_HOME/repository/resources/security/wso2carbon.jks"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The path to the keystore file that is used for securing the HTTP passthrough connection. This validation ensures that erroneous JSON messages will trigger the fault sequence in the Micro Integrator."}</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section></div>

className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The host name of the proxy server."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"proxy_port"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The port number of the proxy server."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"proxy_username"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The password for authenticating the proxy server."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"bypass_hosts"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"A host name or a comma-separated list of host names that should not be sent via the proxy server. ## VFS Transport

<div className={"mb-config-catalog"}><section><div className={"mb-config-options"}><div className={"superfences-tabs"}><input name={"28"} type={"checkbox"} id={"_tab_28"} />{" "}<label className={"tab-selector"} htmlFor={"_tab_28"}><i className={"icon fa fa-code"}></i></label><div className={"superfences-content"}><div className={"mb-config-example"}><pre><code className={"toml"}>{"[transport.vfs]\n\nlistener.enable = true\nlistener.keystore.file_name = \"$ref{keystore.tls.file_name}\" \nlistener.keystore.type = \"$ref{keystore.tls.type}\"\nlistener.keystore.password = \"$ref{keystore.tls.password}\"\nlistener.keystore.key_password = \"$ref{keystore.tls.key_password}\"\nlistener.keystore.alias = \"$ref{keystore.tls.alias}\"\n\nlistener.parameter.customParameter = \"\"\n\nsender.enable = true\nsender.parameter.customParameter = \"\""}</code></pre></div></div><div className={"doc-wrapper"}><div className={"mb-config"}><div className={"config-wrap"}><code>{"[transport.vfs]"}</code>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span><p>{"This configuration header is required for configuring how the Micro Integrator communicates through the "}<a href={"../../install-and-setup/setup/transport-configurations/configuring-transports/#configuring-the-vfs-transport"}>{"VFS transport"}</a>{"."}</p></div><div className={"params-wrap"}><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"listener.enabled"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" boolean "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"true"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"true\" or \"false\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The parameter for enabling the VFS transport listener."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"listener.keystore.file_name"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"MI_HOME/repository/resources/security/wso2carbon.jks"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The path to the keystore file that is used for securing a VFS connection. By default, the keystore file of the primary keystore is enabled for this purpose."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"listener.keystore.type"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"JKS"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"JKS\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The type of the keystore file. By default, the keystore type of the primary keystore is enabled for this purpose."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"listener.keystore.password"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"wso2carbon"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The password of the keystore file that is used for securing a VFS connection. This keystore password is used when accessing the keys in the keystore. By default, the keystore password of the primary keystore is enabled for this purpose."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"listener.keystore.alias"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"wso2carbon"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The alias of the public key corresponding to the private key that is included in the keystore. The public key is used for encrypting data in the Micro Integrator server, which only the corresponding private key can decrypt. The public key is embedded in a digital certificate, and this certificate can be shared over the internet by storing it in a separate trust store file. By default, the alias of the primary keystore is enabled for this purpose."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"listener.keystore.key_password"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"wso2carbon"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The password of the private key that is included in the keystore. The private key is used to decrypt the data that has been encrypted using the keystore's public key. By default, the public key password of the primary keystore is enabled for this purpose."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"sender.enabled"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" boolean "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"wso2carbon"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"true\" or \"false\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The parameter for enabling the VFS transport sender."}</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section></div>

className={"toml"}>{"[[transport.jms.listener]]\n\nname = \"myTopicListener\"\nparameter.initial_naming_factory = \"TopicConnectionFactory\"\nparameter.connection_factory_type = \"topic\"\nparameter.cache_level = \"consumer\"\n\nparameter.naming_security_principal = \"\"\nparameter.naming_security_credential = \"\"\nparameter.transactionality = \"\"\nparameter.transaction_jndi_name = \"\"\nparameter.cache_user_transaction = true\nparameter.session_transaction = true\nparameter.session_acknowledgement = \"AUTO_ACKNOWLEDGE\"\nparameter.jms_spec_version = \"1.1\"\nparameter.username = \"\"\nparameter.password = \"\"\nparameter.destination = \"\"\nparameter.destination_type = \"queue\"\nparameter.default_reply_destination = \"\"\nparameter.default_destination_type = \"queue\"\nparameter.message_selector = \"\"\nparameter.subscription_durable = false\nparameter.durable_subscriber_client_id = \"\"\nparameter.durable_subscriber_name = \"\"\nparameter.pub_sub_local = false\nparameter.receive_timeout = \"1000\"\nparameter.concurrent_consumer = 1\nparameter.max_concurrent_consumer = 1\nparameter.idle_task_limit = 10\nparameter.max_message_per_task = -1\nparameter.initial_reconnection_duration = \"10000\"\nparameter.reconnect_progress_factor = 2\nparameter.max_reconnect_duration = \"3600000\"\nparameter.reconnect_interval = \"3600000\"\nparameter.max_jsm_connection = 10\nparameter.max_consumer_error_retrieve_before_delay = 20\nparameter.consume_error_delay = \"100\"         \nparameter.consume_error_progression = \"2.0\""}</code></pre></div></div><div className={"doc-wrapper"}><div className={"mb-config"}><div className={"toml"}>{"[[transport.blocking.jms.listener]]\n\nname = \"myTopicListener\"\nparameter.initial_naming_factory = \"TopicConnectionFactory\"\nparameter.connection_factory_type = \"topic\"\nparameter.cache_level = \"consumer\"\n\nparameter.naming_security_principal = \"\"\nparameter.naming_security_credential = \"\"\nparameter.transactionality = \"\"\nparameter.transaction_jndi_name = \"\"\nparameter.cache_user_transaction = true\nparameter.session_transaction = true\nparameter.session_acknowledgement = \"AUTO_ACKNOWLEDGE\"\nparameter.jms_spec_version = \"1.1\"\nparameter.username = \"\"\nparameter.password = \"\"\nparameter.destination = \"\"\nparameter.destination_type = \"queue\"\nparameter.default_reply_destination = \"\"\nparameter.default_destination_type = \"queue\"\nparameter.message_selector = \"\"\nparameter.subscription_durable = false\nparameter.durable_subscriber_client_id = \"\"\nparameter.durable_subscriber_name = \"\"\nparameter.pub_sub_local = false\nparameter.receive_timeout = \"1000\"\nparameter.concurrent_consumer = 1\nparameter.max_concurrent_consumer = 1\nparameter.idle_task_limit = 10\nparameter.max_message_per_task = -1\nparameter.initial_reconnection_duration = \"10000\"\nparameter.reconnect_progress_factor = 2\nparameter.max_reconnect_duration = \"3600000\"\nparameter.reconnect_interval = \"3600000\"\nparameter.max_jsm_connection = 10\nparameter.max_consumer_error_retrieve_before_delay = 20\nparameter.consume_error_delay = \"100\"        \nparameter.consume_error_progression = \"2.0\""}</code></pre></div></div><div className={"doc-wrapper"}><div className={"mb-config"}><div ## JMS Transport Sender (blocking mode)

<div className={"mb-config-catalog"}><section><div className={"mb-config-options"}><div className={"superfences-tabs"}><input name={"37"} type={"checkbox"} id={"_tab_37"} />{" "}<label className={"tab-selector"} htmlFor={"_tab_37"}><i className={"icon fa fa-code"}></i></label><div className={"superfences-content"}><div className={"mb-config-example"}><pre><code className={"toml"}>{"[[transport.blocking.jms.sender]]\n\nname = \"myTopicSender\"\nparameter.initial_naming_factory = \"org.apache.activemq.artemis.jndi.ActiveMQInitialContextFactory\"\nparameter.provider_url = \"tcp://localhost:61616\"\nparameter.connection_factory_name = \"TopicConnectionFactory\"\nparameter.connection_factory_type = \"topic\"\nparameter.cache_level = \"producer\"\n\nparameter.naming_security_principal = \"\"\nparameter.naming_security_credential = \"\"\nparameter.transactionality = \"\"\nparameter.transaction_jndi_name = \"\"\nparameter.cache_user_transaction = true\nparameter.session_transaction = true\nparameter.session_acknowledgement = \"AUTO_ACKNOWLEDGE\"\nparameter.jms_spec_version = \"1.1\"\nparameter.username = \"\"\nparameter.password = \"\"\nparameter.destination = \"\"\nparameter.destination_type = \"queue\"\nparameter.default_reply_destination = \"\"\nparameter.default_destination_type = \"queue\"\nparameter.message_selector = \"\"\nparameter.subscription_durable = false\nparameter.durable_subscriber_client_id = \"\"\nparameter.durable_subscriber_name = \"\"\nparameter.pub_sub_local = false\nparameter.receive_timeout = \"1000\"\nparameter.concurrent_consumer = 1\nparameter.max_concurrent_consumer = 1\nparameter.idle_task_limit = 10\nparameter.max_message_per_task = -1\nparameter.initial_reconnection_duration = \"10000\"\nparameter.reconnect_progress_factor = 2\nparameter.max_reconnect_duration = \"3600000\"\nparameter.reconnect_interval = \"3600000\"\nparameter.max_jsm_connection = 10\nparameter.max_consumer_error_retrieve_before_delay = 20\nparameter.consume_error_delay = \"100\"\nparameter.consume_error_progression = \"2.0\"\nparameter.vender_class_loader = false"}</code></pre></div></div><div className={"doc-wrapper"}><div className={"mb-config"}><div className={"config-wrap"}><code>{"[[transport.blocking.jms.sender]]"}</code>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span><p>{"This configuration header groups the parameters that are used to configure the "}<a href={"../../install-and-setup/setup/transport-configurations/configuring-transports/#configuring-the-jms-transport"}>{"JMS transport"}</a>{" sender in blocking mode. You can use the "}<a href={"#jms-transport-sender-non-blocking-mode"}>{"same list of parameters"}</a>{" that are available for the non-blocking JMS sender."}</p></div><div className={"params-wrap"}></div></div></div></div></div></section></div>

## JNDI Queues

<div className={"mb-config-catalog"}><section><div className={"mb-config-options"}><div className={"superfences-tabs"}><input name={"39"} type={"checkbox"} id={"_tab_39"} />{" "}<label className={"tab-selector"} htmlFor={"_tab_39"}><i className={"icon fa fa-code"}></i></label><div className={"superfences-content"}><div className={"mb-config-example"}><pre><code className={"toml"}>{"[transport.jndi.queue]\nJMSMS = \"JMSMS\"\nStockQuotesQueue = \"StockQuotesQueue\""}</code></pre></div></div><div className={"doc-wrapper"}><div className={"mb-config"}><div className={"config-wrap"}><code>{"[transport.jndi.queue]"}</code>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span><p>{"This configuration header is used to specify the list of queues that are defined your JMS broker. ## RabbitMQ Listener

<div className={"mb-config-catalog"}><section><div className={"mb-config-options"}><div className={"superfences-tabs"}><input name={"41"} type={"checkbox"} id={"_tab_41"} />{" "}<label className={"tab-selector"} htmlFor={"_tab_41"}><i className={"icon fa fa-code"}></i></label><div className={"superfences-content"}><div className={"mb-config-example"}><pre><code className={"toml"}>{"[[transport.rabbitmq.listener]]\n\nname = \"rabbitMQListener\"\nparameter.hostname = \"localhost\"\nparameter.port = 5672\nparameter.username = \"guest\"\nparameter.password = \"guest\"\nparameter.connection_factory = \"\"\nparameter.exchange_name = \"amq.direct\"\nparameter.queue_name = \"MyQueue\"\nparameter.queue_auto_ack = false\nparameter.consumer_tag = \"\"\nparameter.channel_consumer_qos = \"\"\nparameter.durable = \"\"\nparameter.queue_exclusive = \"\"\nparameter.queue_auto_delete = \"\"\nparameter.queue_routing_key = \"\"\nparameter.queue_auto_declare = \"\"\nparameter.exchange_auto_declare = \"\"\nparameter.exchange_type = \"\"\nparameter.exchange_durable = \"\"\nparameter.exchange_auto_delete = \"\"\nparameter.message_content_type = \"\"\n\nparameter.retry_interval = \"10s\"\nparameter.retry_count = 5\n\nparameter.ssl_enable = true\nparameter.ssl_version = \"SSL\"\nparameter.keystore_location =\"$ref{keystore.tls.file_name}\"\nparameter.keystore_type = \"$ref{keystore.tls.type}\"\nparameter.keystore_password = \"$ref{keystore.tls.password}\"\nparameter.truststore_file_name =\"$ref{truststore.file_name}\"\nparameter.truststore_type = \"$ref{truststore.type}\"\nparameter.truststore_password = \"$ref{truststore.password}\""}</code></pre></div></div><div className={"doc-wrapper"}><div className={"mb-config"}><div className={"config-wrap"}><code>{"[[transport.rabbitmq.listener]]"}</code>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span><p>{"This configuration header is required if you are configuring WSO2 Micro Integrator to receive messages from a RabbitMQ client. ## RabbitMQ Sender

<div className={"mb-config-catalog"}><section><div className={"mb-config-options"}><div className={"superfences-tabs"}><input name={"42"} type={"checkbox"} id={"_tab_42"} />{" "}<label className={"tab-selector"} htmlFor={"_tab_42"}><i className={"icon fa fa-code"}></i></label><div className={"superfences-content"}><div className={"mb-config-example"}><pre><code className={"toml"}>{"[transport.rabbitmq]\nsender_enable = true\n\n[[transport.rabbitmq.sender]]\nname = \"rabbitMQSender\"\nparameter.hostname = \"localhost\"\nparameter.port = 5672\nparameter.username = \"guest\"\nparameter.password = \"guest\"\nparameter.exchange_name = \"amq.direct\"\nparameter.routing_key = \"MyQueue\"\nparameter.reply_to_name = \"\"\nparameter.queue_delivery_mode = 1 # 1/2\nparameter.exchange_type = \"\"\nparameter.queue_name = \"MyQueue\"\nparameter.queue_durable = false\nparameter.queue_exclusive = false\nparameter.queue_auto_delete = false\nparameter.exchange_durable = \"\"\nparameter.queue_auto_declare = \"\"\nparameter.exchange_auto_declare = \"\"\nparameter.connection_pool_size = 10"}</code></pre></div></div><div className={"doc-wrapper"}><div className={"mb-config"}><div className={"config-wrap"}><code>{"[transport.rabbitmq]"}</code><p>{"This configuration header is required for enabling the RabbitMQ listener in the Micro Integrator. className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The file path to the truststore that stores the trusted digital
