By default, the product's trust store is configured for this purpose."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"sender.truststore_password"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"wso2carbon"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The password of the keystore file that is used as the trust store."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"sender.parameter.customParameter"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Replace 'customParameter' with required parameter name."}</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section></div>


## UDP Transport (non-blocking mode)

<div className={"mb-config-catalog"}><section><div className={"mb-config-options"}><div className={"superfences-tabs"}><input name={"51"} type={"checkbox"} id={"_tab_51"} />{" "}<label className={"tab-selector"} htmlFor={"_tab_51"}><i className={"icon fa fa-code"}></i></label><div className={"superfences-content"}><div className={"mb-config-example"}><pre><code className={"toml"}>{"[transport.udp]\n\nlistener.enable = false\nlistener.parameter.customParameter = \"\"\n\nsender.enable =false               \nsender.parameter.customParameter = \"\""}</code></pre></div></div><div className={"doc-wrapper"}><div className={"mb-config"}><div className={"config-wrap"}><code>{"[transport.udp]"}</code>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span><p>{"This configuration header groups the parameters that configure the Micro Integrator to communicate through the "}<a href={"../../install-and-setup/setup/transport-configurations/configuring-transports/#configuring-the-udp-transport"}>{"UDP transport"}</a>{". Note that the list of parameters given below can be used for the non-blocking transport as well as the "}<a href={"#udp-transport-blocking-mode"}>{"blocking transport"}</a>{"."}</p></div><div className={"params-wrap"}><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"listener.enabled"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" boolean "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"false"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"true\" or \"false\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The parameter for enabling the UDP transport listener."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"sender.enabled"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"false"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"true\" or \"false\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The parameter for enabling the UDP transport sender."}</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section></div>


## UDP Transport (blocking mode)

<div className={"mb-config-catalog"}><section><div className={"mb-config-options"}><div className={"superfences-tabs"}><input name={"52"} type={"checkbox"} id={"_tab_52"} />{" "}<label className={"tab-selector"} htmlFor={"_tab_52"}><i className={"icon fa fa-code"}></i></label><div className={"superfences-content"}><div className={"mb-config-example"}><pre><code className={"toml"}>{"[transport.blocking.udp]\n\nlistener.enable = false\nlistener.parameter.customParameter = \"\"\n\nsender.enable = false        \nsender.parameter.customParameter = \"\""}</code></pre></div></div><div className={"doc-wrapper"}><div className={"mb-config"}><div className={"config-wrap"}><code>{"[transport.blocking.tcp]"}</code>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span><p>{"This configuration header groups the parameters that are used to configure the "}<a href={"../../install-and-setup/setup/transport-configurations/configuring-transports/#configuring-the-udp-transport"}>{"UDP transport"}</a>{" in blocking mode. You can use the "}<a href={"#udp-transport-non-blocking-mode"}>{"same list of parameters"}</a>{" that are available for the non-blocking UDP transport."}</p></div><div className={"params-wrap"}></div></div></div></div></div></section></div>


## Custom Transport Listener

<div className={"mb-config-catalog"}><section><div className={"mb-config-options"}><div className={"superfences-tabs"}><input name={"53"} type={"checkbox"} id={"_tab_53"} />{" "}<label className={"tab-selector"} htmlFor={"_tab_53"}><i className={"icon fa fa-code"}></i></label><div className={"superfences-content"}><div className={"mb-config-example"}><pre><code className={"toml"}>{"[[custom_transport.listener]]\nclass = \"org.wso2.micro.integrator.business.messaging.hl7.transport.HL7TransportListener\"\nprotocol = \"hl7\""}</code></pre></div></div><div className={"doc-wrapper"}><div className={"mb-config"}><div className={"config-wrap"}><code>{"[[custom_transport.listener]]"}</code>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span><p>{"This configuration header groups the parameters that configure the Micro Integrator to receive messages through a "}<a href={"../../install-and-setup/setup/transport-configurations/configuring-transports/#configuring-custom-transports"}>{"custom transport"}</a>{"."}</p></div><div className={"params-wrap"}><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"class"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The class implementing the custom transport. For example, if you are using an HL7 transport listener, use the following class: org.wso2.micro.integrator.business.messaging.hl7.transport.HL7TransportListener."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"protocol"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The transport protocol for the custom implementation. For example: hl7."}</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section></div>


## Custom Transport Sender

<div className={"mb-config-catalog"}><section><div className={"mb-config-options"}><div className={"superfences-tabs"}><input name={"54"} type={"checkbox"} id={"_tab_54"} />{" "}<label className={"tab-selector"} htmlFor={"_tab_54"}><i className={"icon fa fa-code"}></i></label><div className={"superfences-content"}><div className={"mb-config-example"}><pre><code className={"toml"}>{"[[custom_transport.sender]]\nclass = \"org.wso2.micro.integrator.business.messaging.hl7.transport.HL7TransportSender\"\nprotocol = \"hl7\""}</code></pre></div></div><div className={"doc-wrapper"}><div className={"mb-config"}><div className={"config-wrap"}><code>{"[transport.udp]"}</code>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span><p>{"This configuration header groups the parameters that configure the Micro Integrator to send messages through a "}<a href={"../../install-and-setup/setup/transport-configurations/configuring-transports/#configuring-custom-transports"}>{"custom transport"}</a>{"."}</p></div><div className={"params-wrap"}><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"class"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The class implementing the custom transport. For example, if you are using an HL7 transport listener, use the following class: org.wso2.micro.integrator.business.messaging.hl7.transport.HL7TransportSender."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"protocol"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The transport protocol for the custom implementation. For example: hl7."}</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section></div>


## Message Mediation

<div className={"mb-config-catalog"}><section><div className={"mb-config-options"}><div className={"superfences-tabs"}><input name={"55"} type={"checkbox"} id={"_tab_55"} />{" "}<label className={"tab-selector"} htmlFor={"_tab_55"}><i className={"icon fa fa-code"}></i></label><div className={"superfences-content"}><div className={"mb-config-example"}><pre><code className={"toml"}>{"[mediation]\nsynapse.core_threads = 20\nsynapse.max_threads = 100\nsynapse.threads_queue_length = 10\n\nsynapse.global_timeout_interval = \"120000ms\"\n\nsynapse.enable_xpath_dom_failover=true\nsynapse.temp_data_chunk_size=3072\n\nsynapse.command_debugger_port=9005\nsynapse.event_debugger_port=9006\n\nsynapse.script_mediator_pool_size=15\nsynapse.enable_xml_nil=false\nsynapse.disable_auto_primitive_regex = \"^-?(0|[1-9][0-9]*)(\\\\.[0-9]+)?([eE][+-]?[0-9]+)?$\"\nsynapse.disable_custom_replace_regex = \"@@@\"\nsynapse.enable_namespace_declaration = false\nsynapse.build_valid_nc_name = false\nsynapse.enable_auto_primitive = false\nsynapse.json_out_auto_array = false\nsynapse.preserve_namespace_on_xml_to_json=false\nflow.statistics.enable=false\nflow.statistics.capture_all=false\nstatistics.enable_clean=true\nstatistics.clean_interval = \"1000ms\"\nstat.tracer.collect_payloads=false\nstat.tracer.collect_mediation_properties=false\ninbound.core_threads = 20\ninbound.max_threads = 100"}</code></pre></div></div><div className={"doc-wrapper"}><div className={"mb-config"}><div className={"config-wrap"}><code>{"[mediation]"}</code>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span><p>{"This configuration header groups the parameters used for tuning the mediation process (Synapse engine) of the Micro Integrator. These parameters are mainly used when mediators such as Iterate and Clone (which uses the internal thread pools) are used."}</p></div><div className={"params-wrap"}><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"synapse.core_threads"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"20"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The initial number of synapse threads in the pool. This parameter is applicable only if the Iterate and Clone mediators are used to handle a higher load. These mediators use a thread pool to create new threads when processing messages and sending messages in parallal. You can configure the size of the thread pool by this parameter. The number of threads specified via this parameter should be increased as required to balance an increased load. Increasing the value specified for this parameter results in higher performance of the Iterate and Clone mediators."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"synapse.max_threads"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"100"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The maximum number of synapse threads in the pool. This parameter is applicable only if the Iterate and Clone mediators are used to handle a higher load. The number of threads specified for this parameter should be increased as required to balance an increased load."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"synapse.threads_queue_length"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"10"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The length of the queue that is used to hold the runnable tasks that are to be executed by the pool. This parameter is applicable only if the Iterate and Clone mediators are used to handle a higher load. You can specify a finite value as the queue length by giving any positive number. If this parameter is set to (-1) it means that the task queue length is infinite. If the queue length is finite, there can be situations where requests are rejected when the task queue is full and all the cores are occupied. If the queue length is infinite, and if some thread locking happens, the server can go out of memory. Therefore, you need to decide on an optimal value based on the actual load."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"synapse.global_timeout_interval"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"120000"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The maximum number of milliseconds within which a response for the request should be received. A response that arrives after the specified number of seconds cannot be correlated with the request. Hence, a warning will be logged and the request will be dropped. This parameter is also referred to as the time-out handler."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"synapse.enable_xpath_dom_failover"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" boolean "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"false"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"true\" or \"false\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"If this parameter is set to true, the Micro Integrator can switch to XPATH 2.0. This parameter can be set to false if XPATH 2.0 evaluations cause performance degradation. The Micro Integrator uses the Saxon Home Edition when implementing XPATH 2.0 functionalities, and thus supports all the functions that are shipped with it. For more information on the supported functions, see the Saxon Documentation."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"synapse.temp_data_chunk_size"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"3072"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The message size that can be processed by the Micro Integrator."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"synapse.script_mediator_pool_size"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"15"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"When using externally referenced scripts, this parameter specifies the size of the script engine pool that should be used per script mediator. The script engines from this pool are used for externally referenced script execution where updates to external scripts on an engine currently in use may otherwise not be thread safe. It is recommended to keep this value at a reasonable size since there will be a pool per externally referenced script."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"synapse.preserve_namespace_on_xml_to_json"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" boolean "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"false"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"true\" or \"false\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Preserves the namespace declarations in the JSON output during XML to JSON message transformations."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"flow.statistics.enable"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" boolean "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"false"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"true\" or \"false\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Set this property to true and enable statistics for the required integration artifact to record information such as the following: <ul><li>The time spent on each mediator.</li><li>The time spent on processing each message.</li><li>The fault count of a single message flow.</li></ul>"}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"flow.statistics.capture_all"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" boolean "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"false"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"true\" or \"false\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Set this property to 'true' and set the flow.statistics.enable property also to 'true'. This will enable mediation statistics for all the integration artifacts by default. If you set this property to 'false', you need to set the flow.statistics.enable property to 'true' and manually enable statistics for the required integration artifact."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"statistics.enable_clean"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" boolean "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"true"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"true\" or \"false\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"If this parameter is set to true, all the existing statistics would be cleared before processing a request. This is recommended if you want to increase the processing speed."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"stat.tracer.collect_payloads"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" boolean "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"false"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"true\" or \"false\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Set this property to true and enable tracing for the required integration artifact to record the message payload before and after the message mediation performed by individual mediators."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"stat.tracer.collect_mediation_properties"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" boolean "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"false"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"true\" or \"false\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Set this property to true and enable tracing for the required integration artifact to record the following information:<ul><li>Message context properties.</li><li>Message transport-scope properties.</li></ul>"}</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section></div>

## Observability - OpenTelemetry

<div className={"mb-config-catalog"}><section><div className={"mb-config-options"}><div className={"superfences-tabs"}><input name={"56"} type={"checkbox"} id={"_tab_56"} />{" "}<label className={"tab-selector"} htmlFor={"_tab_56"}><i className={"icon fa fa-code"}></i></label><div className={"superfences-content"}><div className={"mb-config-example"}><pre><code className={"toml"}>{"[opentelemetry]\nenable = false\nlogs = false\ntype = \"otlp\"\nhost = \"localhost\"\nport = 4318\n\n[[opentelemetry.properties]]\nname = \"api-key\"\nvalue = \"your-api-key\"\n"}</code></pre></div></div><div className={"doc-wrapper"}><div className={"mb-config"}><div className={"config-wrap"}><code>{"[opentelemetry]"}</code><p>{"This configuration header groups the parameters used for enabling and configuring OpenTelemetry tracing in the WSO2 Integrator: MI. OpenTelemetry allows distributed tracing for debugging, observing, and identifying bottlenecks in message flows."}</p></div><div className={"params-wrap"}><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"enable"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" boolean "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"false"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"true\" or \"false\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Enables or disables OpenTelemetry tracing for the MI server. Set this to true to enable distributed tracing capabilities."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"logs"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" boolean "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"false"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"true\" or \"false\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Enables or disables logging for OpenTelemetry traces. When enabled, tracing information is logged for debugging purposes."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"type"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"jaeger"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"jaeger\", \"zipkin\", \"log\", \"otlp\", or custom type name"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Specifies the type of OpenTelemetry exporter to use. Options include jaeger (Jaeger tracing), zipkin (Zipkin tracing), log (log-based tracing), otlp (OpenTelemetry Protocol for APMs like NewRelic, Elastic), or a custom exporter type."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"host"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"localhost"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"Valid hostname or IP address"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The hostname or IP address of the tracing backend endpoint. This parameter is optional if the url parameter is specified. Used with Jaeger, Zipkin, or OTLP exporters."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"port"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" integer "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"14250"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"Valid port number"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The port number of the tracing backend endpoint. This parameter is optional if the url parameter is specified. Common ports: 14250 for Jaeger, 9411 for Zipkin, 4318 for OTLP HTTP, 4317 for OTLP gRPC."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"url"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"Valid URL"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The complete URL of the tracing backend endpoint. This parameter can be used instead of specifying separate host and port parameters. Example: http://localhost:4318 for OTLP."}</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section></div>

Additional custom properties can be configured using the `[[opentelemetry.properties]]` array configuration:

<div className={"mb-config-catalog"}><section><div className={"mb-config-options"}><div className={"superfences-tabs"}><input name={"57"} type={"checkbox"} id={"_tab_57"} />{" "}<label className={"tab-selector"} htmlFor={"_tab_57"}><i className={"icon fa fa-code"}></i></label><div className={"superfences-content"}><div className={"mb-config-example"}><pre><code className={"toml"}>{"[[opentelemetry.properties]]\nname = \"api-key\"\nvalue = \"your-api-key-value\"\n\n[[opentelemetry.properties]]\nname = \"custom-header\"\nvalue = \"custom-value\"\n"}</code></pre></div></div><div className={"doc-wrapper"}><div className={"mb-config"}><div className={"config-wrap"}><code>{"[[opentelemetry.properties]]"}</code><p>{"This configuration allows you to specify custom headers and properties for OpenTelemetry exporters. This is particularly useful when connecting to APM providers that require authentication headers or other custom metadata. You can define multiple property entries by repeating the "}<code>{"[[opentelemetry.properties]]"}</code>{" section."}</p></div><div className={"params-wrap"}><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"name"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"Property or header name"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The name of the custom property or header to be sent with OpenTelemetry traces. For example, api-key for authentication headers."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"value"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"Property or header value"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The value of the custom property or header. For example, the API key value for authentication."}</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section></div>

## REST API - CORS Configuration

To enable CORS for the [REST APIs](../develop/creating-artifacts/creating-an-api.mdx) globally, you need to configure the API's CORS settings in the `deployment.toml` file:

<div className={"mb-config-catalog"}><section><div className={"mb-config-options"}><div className={"superfences-tabs"}><input name={"56"} type={"checkbox"} id={"_tab_56"} />{" "}<label className={"tab-selector"} htmlFor={"_tab_56"}><i className={"icon fa fa-code"}></i></label><div className={"superfices-content"}><div className={"mb-config-example"}><pre><code className={"toml"}>{"[synapse_properties]\n'synapse.rest.CORSConfig.enabled' = \"true\"\n'synapse.rest.CORSConfig.Access-Control-Allow-Origin' = \"https://test.com\"\n'synapse.rest.CORSConfig.Access-Control-Allow-Headers' = \"content-type, Accept, Authorization\"\n'synapse.rest.CORSConfig.Access-Control-Allow-Methods' = \"GET, POST, DELETE, PUT, PATCH, OPTIONS\"\n"}</code></pre></div></div><div className={"doc-wrapper"}><div className={"mb-config"}><div className={"config-wrap"}><code>{"[synapse_properties]"}</code>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span><p>{"This configuration header is required for configuring CORS settings for REST APIs globally."}</p></div><div className={"params-wrap"}><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"synapse.rest.CORSConfig.enabled"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"true"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"\"true\" or \"false\""}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Enable/Disable CORS for the REST APIs at the server level."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"synapse.rest.CORSConfig.Access-Control-Allow-Origin"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-optional"}>{"Optional"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Specifies the allowed origins for CORS requests. Use '*' to allow all origins or specify a specific URL."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"synapse.rest.CORSConfig.Access-Control-Allow-Headers"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-optional"}>{"Optional"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Specifies the allowed headers for CORS requests. Provide a comma-separated list of header names."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"synapse.rest.CORSConfig.Access-Control-Allow-Methods"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-optional"}>{"Optional"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Specifies the allowed methods for CORS requests. Provide a comma-separated list of HTTP methods (e.g., GET, POST)."}</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section></div>

## External Valut Configurations

<div className={"mb-config-catalog"}><section><div className={"mb-config-options"}><div className={"superfences-tabs"}><input name={"57"} type={"checkbox"} id={"_tab_57"} />{" "}<label className={"tab-selector"} htmlFor={"_tab_57"}><i className={"icon fa fa-code"}></i></label><div className={"superfences-content"}><div className={"mb-config-example"}><pre><code className={"toml"}>{"#Static Token Authentication\n\n[[external_vault]]\nname = \"hashicorp\" # required\naddress = \"http://127.0.0.1:8200\" # required\nrootToken = \"ROOT_TOKEN\" # required\ncachableDuration = \"15000\"\nengineVersion = \"2\"\n# If namespace is used, apply the namespace value:\nnamespace = \"NAMESPACE\"\n# If HashiCorp vault server is hosted in HTTPS protocol, apply below fields\ntrustStoreFile = \"${carbon.home}/repository/resources/security/client-truststore.jks\"\nkeyStoreFile = \"${carbon.home}/repository/resources/security/wso2carbon.jks\"\nkeyStorePassword = \"KEY_STORE_PASSWORD\"\n\n#AppRole Authentication\n\n[[external_vault]]\nname = \"hashicorp\" # required\naddress = \"http://127.0.0.1:8200\" # required\nroleId = \"ROLE_ID\" # required\nsecretId = \"SECRET_ID\" # required\ncachableDuration = \"15000\"\nengineVersion = \"2\"\n# If namespace is used, apply the namespace value:\nnamespace = \"NAMESPACE\"\n# If HashiCorp vault server is hosted in HTTPS protocol, apply below fields\ntrustStoreFile = \"${carbon.home}/repository/resources/security/client-truststore.jks\"\nkeyStoreFile = \"${carbon.home}/repository/resources/security/wso2carbon.jks\"\nkeyStorePassword = \"KEY_STORE_PASSWORD\"\n\n#LDAP Authentication\n\n[[external_vault]]\nname = \"hashicorp\" # required\naddress = \"http://127.0.0.1:8200\" # required\nldapUsername = \"USERNAME\" # required\nldapPassword = \"PASSWORD\" # required\ncachableDuration = \"15000\"\nengineVersion = \"2\"\n# If HashiCorp vault server is hosted in HTTPS protocol, apply below fields\ntrustStoreFile = \"${carbon.home}/repository/resources/security/client-truststore.jks\"\nkeyStoreFile = \"${carbon.home}/repository/resources/security/wso2carbon.jks\"\nkeyStorePassword = \"KEY_STORE_PASSWORD\""}</code></pre></div></div><div className={"doc-wrapper"}><div className={"mb-config"}><div className={"config-wrap"}><code>{"[[external_vault]]"}</code>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span><p>{"This configuration header is required for configuring an external vault for secrets. Read more about "}<a href={"../../install-and-setup/setup/security/using-hashicorp-secrets"}>{"using HashiCorp sercrets"}</a>{"."}</p></div><div className={"params-wrap"}><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"name"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"hashicorp"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The name of the vault. For example, specify 'hashicorp' when connecting to the HashiCorp vault."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"address"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span>{" "}<span className={"badge-required"}>{"Required"}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The URL for connecting to the vault."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"rootToken"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Specify the root token generated from the HashiCorp server. This is only applicable if static token authentication is used when connecting the Micro Integrator to the HashiCorp server."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"roleId"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Specify the role ID generated from HashiCorp. The secret ID and role ID you specify in the deployment.toml file will internally generate a token and authenticate the HashiCorp server connection. The role ID is only applicable if AppRole Pull authentication is used when connecting the Micro Integrator to the HashiCorp server."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"secretId"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Specify the secret ID generated from HashiCorp. The secret ID and role ID you sepecify in the deployment.toml file will internally generate a token and authenticate the HashiCorp server connection. The secret ID you generate in HashiCorp may expire. If that happens, you can renew the security token. The secret ID is only applicable if AppRole Pull authentication is used when connecting the Micro Integrator to the HashiCorp server."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"cachableDuration"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"15000"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"All resources fetched from the HashiCorp vault would be cached for this number of milliseconds."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"engineVersion"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"2"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The version of the HashiCorp secret engine."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"namespace"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"Namespace support is available only in the Enterprise edition of HashiCorp. The namespace value specified here applies globally to HashiCorp secrets in all synapse configurations."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"trustStoreFile"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"${carbon.home}/repository/resources/security/client-truststore.jks"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The keystore file (trust store) that is used to store the digital certificates that the Micro Integrator trusts for SSL communication."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"keyStoreFile"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"${carbon.home}/repository/resources/security/wso2carbon.jks"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"This keystore used for SSL handshaking when the Micro Integrator communicates with the HashiCorp server."}</p></div></div></div><div className={"param"}><div className={"param-name"}><span className={"param-name-wrap"}><code>{"keyStorePassword"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-info"}><div><p><span className={"param-type string"}>{" string "}</span></p><div className={"param-default"}><span className={"param-default-value"}>{"Default: "}<code>{"wso2carbon"}</code></span></div><div className={"param-possible"}><span className={"param-possible-values"}>{"Possible Values: "}<code>{"-"}</code></span></div></div><div className={"param-description"}><p>{"The password of the keystore file that is used for SSL communication. If you are using the default keystore file in the Micro Integrator, the default password is 'wso2carbon'."}</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section></div>
